## Document 004: 115年8月海關進出口貿易初步統計 (2026-09-09)

Report source: MOF
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

115 年 8 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
8 月出口 824.0 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 9.4%，較上年
同月增 41.0%(按新臺幣計算增 52.6%)；累計 1 至 8 月出口 5,743.4
億美元，較上年同期增 44.2%(按新臺幣計算增 46.5%)。 
（二）進口 
8 月進口 601.0 億美元，為歷年單月第 3 高，較上月增 3.4%，較上
年同月增 44.3%(按新臺幣計算增 56.2%)；累 計1 至 8 月進口 4,374.3
億美元，較上年同期增 40.4%(按新臺幣計算增 42.4%)。 
（三）出入超 
8 月出超 223.0 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 55.2 億美元；
累計 1 至 8 月出超 1,369.1 億美元，較上年同期增 500.5 億美元。

### Page 2

2 
二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
受惠於人工智慧(AI)、 高效能運算及雲端服務等應用持續擴增，相關
產品出貨維持強勁， 加上價格攀升效 應，8月出口較上年同月增 239.7
億美元(+41.0%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 電子零組件出口 322.2 億美元(歷年單月新高)， 較上年同月增118.3
億美元(+58.0%)，其中積體電路增 115.5 億美元(+60.0%)、印刷電
路增 1.4 億美元(+29.9%)、 電容器及電阻器增0.7 億美元(+24.9%)。 
2. 資通與視聽產品出口 331.0 億美元，較上年同月增 97.6 億美元
(+41.8%)，其中電腦及其附屬單元增68.7 億美元(+40.4%)、儲存媒
體增 19.1 億美元(+2.4 倍)、 交換器及路由器增9.7 億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
3. 礦產品出口 20.0 億美元，較上年同月增 8.3 億美元(+70.2%)，其
中石油煉製品增 7.8 億美元(+74.3%)。 
4. 基本金屬及其製品出口 29.6 億美元，較上年同月增 5.4 億美元
(+22.4%)，其中銅及其製品增 6.1 億美元(+1.5 倍)，鋼鐵及其製品
減 1.1 億美元(-8.7%)。 
5. 機械出口 26.8 億美元，較上年同月增 3.6 億美元(+15.2%)，其中
生產半導體等機械增 1.9 億美元(+42.1%)、未列名具特殊功能機械
增 0.4 億美元(+39.0%)。

累計 1 至 8 月出口較上年同期增 1,759.8 億美元(+44.2%)，以資通與
視聽產品增 1,023.7 億美元(+68.5%)、電子零組件增 629.5 億美元
(+45.8%)增加較多。

生產等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
由於 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口衍生需求之帶動，積體電路及資通產
品進口維持強勁，加上輸入物價明顯上漲，8 月總進口較上年同月增
184.5 億美元(+44.3%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口408.4 億美元， 較上年同月增129.4億美元(+46.4%)，
其中電子零組件增 82.5 億美元(+62.6%)、礦產品增 16.9 億美元
(+38.4%)、電腦之零附件增 14.6 億美元(+84.4%)、基本金屬增 4.5
億美元(+21.8%)、塑化原料增 4.0 億美元(+16.2%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 130.6 億美元， 較上年同月增 38.4 億美元(+41.6%)，
其中資通設備增 27.2 億美元(+1.2 倍)、半導體設備增 2.8 億美元
(+7.3%)、交通運輸設備增 2.8 億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
3. 消費品進口 55.7 億美元，較上年同月增 16.0 億美元(+40.5%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增12.1 億美元(+3.7 倍)、藥粧用品增 1.2
億美元(+17.4%)、手機增 1.1 億美元(+78.1%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
8 月對陸港、日本出口創新高，對美國、東協為單月第 3 高 
1. 對美國出口 236.7 億美元，較上年同月增 40.6 億美元(+20.7%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 23.0 億美元(+14.6%)、電子零組件增 11.6
億美元(+1.5 倍)、電機產品增 1.7 億美元(+30.1%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 211.5 億美元，較上年同月增 59.7 億美元
(+39.3%)，其中電子零組件增 47.4 億美元(+45.6%)、資通與視聽
產品增 6.7 億美元(+42.9%)、 基本金屬及其製品增4.6 億美元(+1.2
倍)。 
3. 對東協出口 151.3 億美元，較上年同月增 44.9 億美元(+42.2%)，
其中電子零組件增 25.3 億美元(+50.3%)、資通與視聽產品增 12.6
億美元(+41.1%)、礦產品增 4.6 億美元(+1.5 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 44.5 億美元，較上年同月增 11.5 億美元(+34.8%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 6.6 億美元(+57.0%)、電子零組件增 4.0 億美
元(+1.1 倍)，基本金屬及其製品減 1.4 億美元(-31.0%)。 
5. 對日本出口 36.6 億美元，較上年同月增8.9 億美元(+32.0%)，其中
電子零組件增 8.9 億美元(+76.7%)、 化學品增0.5 億美元(+32.3%)，
資通與視聽產品減 0.6 億美元(-13.9%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 8 月對美國出口 1,803.2 億美元，較上年同期增 631.6 億美
元(+53.9%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,394.7 億美元，增 307.5 億美
元(+28.3%)， 對東協出口1,150.7 億美元 ， 增372.3 億美元(+47.8%)，
對歐洲出口 376.6 億美元 ， 增130.7 億美元(+53.1%)， 對日本出口247.0
億美元，增 54.7 億美元(+28.4%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
8 月自中國大陸與香港進口 126.8 億美元 ， 較上年同月增47.6 億美元
(+60.2%)，自東協進口 106.9 億美元，增46.7 億美元(+77.6%)，自南
韓進口 91.7 億美元，增29.6 億美元(+47.7%)，自日本進口 57.0 億美
元，增 11.2 億美元(+24.4%)，自美國進口 53.9 億美元，增 14.3 億美
元(+35.9%)，自歐洲進口 49.5 億美元，增 6.2 億美元(+14.3%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 8 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增75.4%、
自南韓增 63.2%、自中國大陸與香港增 40.5%、自美國增 24.4%、自
日本增 24.1%、自歐洲增 12.8%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）今年 1-7 月主要國家/地區外貿景氣普遍上升，南韓(1-8 月)、香港、
新加坡出口動能尤為強勁，分別年增 52.8%、46.6%、38.3%；中國
大陸(1-8 月)、美國出口分別年增 19.3%及 14.6%，德國(1-6 月)、日
本各年增 10.9%、8.1%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步上揚，累計 1-7 月以香港進口年增 48.1%最
為顯著， 新加坡、 中國大陸(1-8 月)分別年增 37.0%、27.0%，南韓(1-8
月)、德國(1-6 月)各增 18.7%、11.7%，日本、美國各增 6.5%、1.2%。

圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）得益於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及 雲端服務等應用 需求強勁， 加
以外銷產品價格攀升，8 月出口 824.0 億美元，創歷年單月新高，年
增 41.0%，連續 34 個月正成長；進口 601.0 億美元，年增 44.3%，
主因 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口衍生需求擴張 ，及農工原料行情上
漲；出、進口互抵後，出超 223.0 億美元，亦創單月新高 。累計 1
至 8 月出口年增 44.2%、進口增 40.4%。
（二）8 月出口主要貨品以電子零組件、資通與視聽產品貢獻最多，年增率
分別為 58.0%及 41.8%，前者受惠 AI 商機及記憶體外銷價量齊揚，規
模值創單月新高，兩類併計增 49.3%，其餘貨類平均增 16.3%，以礦
產品隨油品售價上漲，年增 70.2%較為突出，基本金屬及其製品、電
機產品各增 22.4%、22.3%。累計 1 至 8 月資通與視聽產品出口值已
超越歷年水準，與電子零組件合計占總出口比重達 78.7%。

（三）8 月對主要市場出口同呈雙位數成長，其中對中國大陸與香港、日本
受電子零組件外銷強勁推升，出口值同創單月新高，各年增 39.3%、
32.0%，對東協、歐洲、美國分別增 42.2%、34.8%、20.7%；五大市
場之外，對墨西哥及澳大利亞出口皆增逾 2 倍。累計 1 至 8 月對美
國、歐洲出口各年增 53.9%、53.1%，對東協年增 47.8%，對日本、
陸港各增 28.4%、28.3%，對五大市場出口規模均為歷年同期最佳。
（四）展望未來，地緣政治衝突 及美國關稅政策變化等不確定 因素，持續
干擾全球經濟成長力道，惟隨人工智慧應用 不斷深化，算力需求攀
升，國際雲端服務業者加速建置 AI 基礎設施，加以國內半導體及伺
服器等供應鏈具競爭優勢，均有助支撐我國外銷動能，下半年出口
可望維持成長態勢。

## Document 010: 2026-09-25_［日銀短観予測］堅調なAI関連需要を受け、製造業の景況感が改善すると予想_72bbfa5b

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-09-25_［日銀短観予測］堅調なAI関連需要を受け、製造業の景況感が改善すると予想_72bbfa5b.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-09-25
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

堅調なAI関連需要を受け、製造 
業の景況感が改善すると予想

○ 業況判断ＤＩ予測：大企業・製造業   ＋25％Pt（6月調査から3ポイント改善） 
                  大企業・非製造業 ＋37％Pt（6月調査から横ばい） 
○ 2026年度設備投資計画(全規模・全産業)予測：前年度比＋7.8％（6月調査：同＋6.8％）

大企業・製造業の業況判断は
改善、大企業・非製造業の業
況判断は横ばいを予想 
10月1日公表の日銀短観（9月調査）では、大企業・製造業の業況判断DI
が＋25％Ptと、6月調査（＋22％Pt）から3ポイントの改善を予想（図表1）
する。関連指標では、7～9月期法人企業景気予測調査において、大企業の
「貴社の景況判断BSI」は製造業を中心に改善したほか、9月ロイター短観で
は、半導体やデータセンター（DC）関連の受注が好調との声が目立った。AI
関連需要が旺盛ななか、生産用機械や化学、非鉄金属などの関連業種で幅広
く景況感が改善すると見込む。

### Page 2

2

実際、AI関連財輸出の増勢は拡大している（図表2） 。半導体・電子部品の
輸出は、メモリ価格上昇の恩恵を受けて前年比で大幅プラスとなっているほ
か、足元では半導体製造・検査機器の輸出数量が急増している。その他、DC
に使われる冷却装置などの設備や、半導体製造に不可欠な原材料・素材の輸
出も増加するなど、輸出品目の裾野は広がっているようだ。こうしたもと、
AI関連財の生産指数は2025年9月比＋11.3％と大幅に上昇（図表3）してお
り、AI関連財の増産が鉱工業生産全体のけん引役になっている。 
また、足元にかけて円安基調が継続していることも、こうした輸出志向の
強い製造業種の景況感を下支えしている。 
大企業・非製造業の業況判断DIは＋37％Pt（6月調査：＋37％Pt）と、高
水準が継続する見込みだ。 
既往の資源高を背景に、卸売業の景況感が堅調となることが期待されるほ
か、堅調な投資需要を背景に、建設業でも良好な景況感が続きそうだ。さら
に、不動産価格や賃料の上昇を受けて、不動産業も好調を維持するとみてい
る。 
宿泊・飲食サービス等のインバウンド関連業種の景況感も底堅い結果が予
想される。訪日外客数については、短期的にはやや増勢が鈍化している。中
東情勢悪化を背景とした航空便の減便や、燃油サーチャージの上昇を受けた
航空運賃の負担増などが背景にある。日中対立を受けて中国の訪日外客数が
大幅減となっている状況も不変だ。もっとも、韓国や台湾など近隣諸国から
の訪日外客数は大幅な増加が続いており、全体としてみれば、大幅な腰折れ
は回避されている。

### Page 3

3

なお、製造業・非製造業に共通する要因として、中東情勢悪化を受けた石
化製品価格など原材料価格の上昇や人件費上昇といったコスト増による業績
下押し圧力は、日本企業全体としてみれば限定的なものに留まるとみてい
る。大企業やサプライチェーンの上流企業を中心に価格転嫁が進んでおり、
コスト増を一定程度吸収できている。上述の法人企業景気予測調査における
2026年度の経常利益計画が製造業・非製造業ともに上方修正され、増益見込
みに転じていることも、全体としての企業景況感が底堅さを維持するとみて
いる根拠の一つである。ただし、中小企業や、消費者に近いサプライチェー
ンの下流企業では十分な価格転嫁が進まず、景況感の下押し圧力となること
が予想される。 
先行きの業況判断DIは、製造業・非製造業ともに企業規模を問わず悪化が
見込まれる。もっとも、先行きの業況判断DIは現在対比で下振れしやすいク
セのある統計である。中東情勢が引き続き予断を許さないことなど、懸念材
料はあるが、実態としては、日本企業は先行きの景気をさほど悲観的に見て
いない可能性が高い。 
2026年度も、引き続き旺盛な
投資計画が示される見通し 
2026年度の設備投資計画 （全規模 ・ 全産業） は同＋7.8％（6月調査：＋6.8％）
と上方修正を予測する。従来からの旺盛な投資需要に加え、政府が7月以降開
始した「大胆な投資促進税制」に基づく投資支援策も、民間企業の投資需要
を下支えする公算が大きい。中東情勢悪化に伴う不確実性が前回調査時点か
ら幾分後退しているとみられることも、ポジティブな材料だ。 
日本政策投資銀行が公表した 「全国設備投資計画調査（2026年6月）」に よ
れば、化学・電気機械・非鉄金属・電力など、幅広い業種で旺盛なAI関連需
要に対応することを目的とした投資が計画されており、投資のけん引役とな
ることが期待される。 
不動産やサービス業では、ホテル開発や娯楽施設の高付加価値化といった
インバウンド関連投資も好調のようだ。自動車では、EV関連投資が一部減退
し、2025年度計画比では控えめな計画になることが予想されるものの、全体
としてみれば旺盛な投資需要が示されるだろう。

## Document 055: 2026年9月日銀短観

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月日銀短観.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和
証券グループ本社を親会社とする大和証券グループの会社です。内容に関する一切の権利は㈱大和総研にあります。無断での複製・転載・転送等はご遠慮ください。

2026 年10 月1 日 全 8 頁

2026 年 9 月日銀短観 
AI 需要で製造業は改善も、先行きはコスト増や金利上昇で業況悪化

経済調査部 エコノミスト 中村 華奈子

[要約] 
 2026 年 9 月日銀短観では、大企業製造業の業況判断DI（最近）は＋24％pt（前回差＋
2％pt）に改善した一方、大企業非製造業は＋35％pt（同▲2％pt）へと悪化した。製
造業では、旺盛な AI 関連需要が景況感を押し上げた。非製造業では、燃料費や人件費
などのコスト増が重石となり、 「宿泊・飲食サービス」や「小売」など個人消費に関連
する業種を中心に業況が悪化した。 
 業況判断 DI（先行き）は、大企業製造業で＋21％pt（今回差▲3％pt） 、大企業非製造
業で＋30％pt（同▲5％pt）といずれも悪化が見込まれている。中東情勢に収束の兆し
が見られないなか、原油価格の高止まりに伴うコスト増 への懸念や、金利上昇による
資金調達コスト増大や需要の下押しへの警戒感から、幅広い業種で慎重な見方が広が
った。 
 2026 年度の設備投資計画（全規模全産業、含む土地、ソフトウェアと研究開発投資額
は含まない）は前年度比＋7.6％へと上方修正されたが、おおむね例年通りの修正幅だ
った。省力化投資や更新投資、能力増強 投資等への意欲は引き続き強いとみられ、大
企業では製造業・非製造業ともに前年度比 2 桁増の計画が維持されたほか、中小企業
でも、非製造業を中心に前回調査から上方修正された。 
 企業の販売価格の見通しと物価全般の見通しはいずれも高水準にあり、企業の価格設
定行動は今後も積極的な状態が続くとみられる。資金繰り判断 DI（最近）や金融機関
の貸出態度判断 DI（最近）は横ばい圏で推移しており、金融環境はおおむね緩和的な
状態が維持されているといえる。もっとも、借入金利水準判断 DI（最近）は大企業・
中小企業ともに上昇し、先行きも一段の上昇が見込まれるなど、利上げの影響が企業
にも広がりつつある。

### Page 2

1

2 / 8

【業況判断 DI（最近） 】AI関連需要が追い風となる一方、コスト増で非製造業は悪化 
2026 年 9 月の日銀短観では、大企業製造業の業況判断DI（最近）は＋24％pt（前回差＋2％
pt） 、大企業非製造業では＋35％pt（同▲2％pt）となった。製造業では、旺盛なAI関連需要が
景況感を押し上げた。非製造業では、燃料費や人件費などのコスト増加が収益の重石となり、
個人消費に関連する業種を中心に業況が悪化した。 
大企業製造業のうち、 「素材業種」の業況判断DI（最近）は前回差＋7％ptだった。原材料コ
スト上昇分の価格転嫁が進展し、「石油・石炭製品」 （同＋27％pt）や「鉄鋼」 （同＋12％pt）
などの業況判断 DI（最近）が上昇したほか、 「窯業・土石製品」 （同＋11％pt）や「非鉄金属」
（同＋9％pt） 、 「化学」 （同＋6％pt）といったAI関連の素材を扱う業種でも業況が改善した。 
「加工業種」の業況判断 DI（最近）は前回差＋2％pt だった。 「生産用機械」 （同＋7％pt）や
「電気機械」 （同＋4％pt）では、半導体関連を中心とする AI 関連需要を追い風に業況が改善し
た。他方、 「食料品」 （同▲6％pt）では原材料・エネルギーコストの上昇が重石となったとみ
られるほか、 「造船・重機等」 （同▲3％pt）や「自動車」 （同▲1％pt）でも業況判断DI（最近）
が低下した。 
大企業非製造業では、「宿泊・飲食サービス」 （前回差▲8％pt）や「小売」 （同▲4％pt）、
「対個人サービス」 （同▲3％pt）など、個人消費に関連する業種で業況判断 DI（最近）が低下
した。燃料費や人件費の上昇に加え、訪日客数の停滞などが景況感を下押ししたとみられる。
また、 「運輸・郵便」 （同▲4％pt）では燃料費の上昇、 「物品賃貸」 （同▲4％pt）では金利上昇
に伴う調達
コストの増加が業況の悪化につながった可能性がある。

【業況判断 DI（先行き）】コスト高や金利上昇への警戒感から幅広い業種で悪化見込み 
大企業製造業の業況判断 DI（先行き）は＋21％pt（今回差▲3％pt） 、大企業非製造業は＋
30％pt（同▲5％pt）と、いずれも悪化が見込まれている。 
大企業製造業では、 「素材業種」が＋15％pt（今回差▲10％pt） 、 「加工業種」が＋23％pt
（同▲2％pt）と素材業種の低下幅が大きい。中東情勢に収束の兆しが見られないなか、原油価
格の高止まりに伴うコスト増や原材料の供給制約に対する警戒感が重石となったとみられる。
一方、加工業種では好調なAI関連需要もあってDIの低下は小幅だった。 
大企業非製造業では、 「物品賃貸」 （今回差▲19％pt） 、 「不動産」 （同▲11％pt）、「 建 設 」（ 同
▲8％pt）など幅広い業種で業況判断 DI（先行き）が低下した。物品賃貸や不動産では、金利
上昇による資金調達コストの上昇や、それに伴う設備・住宅需要の下押しが懸念されたとみら
れる。物価上昇に伴う家計の実質購買力の低下や最低賃金引き上げに伴う人件費負担の増加に
対する懸念も、幅広い業種で慎重な見方が広がった一因と考えられる。

【売上高・経常利益計画】全規模全産業は増収増益も、円安の影響には注意が必要 
2026 年度における全規模全産業の売上高は前年度比＋4.1％の増収、経常利益は同＋2.2％の
増益が見込まれている。2026年度の想定為替レート（円/ドル、全規模・全産業）は154.23 円
であり、足元の水準（158 円/ドル台）よりも円高水準にある。今後、企業の想定為替レートが
円安方向へ修正されると、輸出比率の高い製造業を中心に、売上高や経常利益計画が上方修正
される可能性がある。他方、輸入燃料や原材料への依存度が高い運輸・郵便や食料品などの業
種では、円安に伴うコスト増加を受けて経常利益計画が下方修正されるリスクにも注意が必要
だ。

【需給・価格判断】中小企業では先行きの価格転嫁の進展が見込まれる 
大企業の「国内での製商品・サービス需給判断 DI（最近） 」は、製造業で▲1％pt（前回差＋
3％pt） 、非製造業で＋4％pt（同▲2％pt）だった。先行きは、製造業で▲1％pt（今回差±0％
pt） 、非製造業で＋3％pt（同▲1％pt）と見込まれている。 
大企業の「海外での製商品需給判断 DI（最近）」は、 「素材業種」で▲8％pt（前回差±0％
pt） 、 「加工業種」で＋3％pt（同＋3％pt）であった。 「海外での製商品需給判断DI（先行き）」
は、 「素材業種」と「加工業種」のいずれも今回差＋1％pt と改善した。 
大企業の「仕入価格判断 DI（最近） 」は、製造業で＋59％pt（前回差▲3％pt） 、非製造業で
＋54％pt（同▲2％pt）であった。先行きでは、製造業が＋55％pt（今回差▲4％pt） 、非製造
業は＋54％pt（同±0％pt）となった。大企業の「販売価格判断 DI（最近） 」は、製造業が＋
40％pt（前回差±0％pt） 、非製造業が＋38％pt（同▲2％pt）で あった。先行きは、製造業が
＋40％pt（今回差±0％pt） 、非製造業が＋40％pt（同＋2％pt）となった。 
企業の
交易条件（販売価格判断 DI と仕入価格判断 DI の差）を確認すると、中小企業では製
造業、非製造業ともに先行きの仕入価格判断DI の変化幅を販売価格判断 DI の上昇幅が上回っ
ており、価格転嫁の進展が見込まれる。 
企業の物価見通し（全規模全産業）を見ると、販売価格の見通しと物価全般の見通しはいず
れも高水準にある。企業はこれまでのコスト増を持続的な物価上昇圧力として認識している可
能性があり、今後も企業の価格設定行動を後押しするとみられる。

ない）は前年度比＋7.6％と、おおむね例年通りの修正幅であった。省力化投資や更新投資に

された。

【雇用判断】雇用の不足感は中小企業を中心に一段と強まる見込み 
雇用の過不足感を示す「雇用人員判断 DI」 （最近）は、大企業製造業で▲22％pt（前回差▲
1％pt）、大企業非製造業で▲37％pt（同▲1％pt）だった。中小企業では、製造業で▲32 ％pt
（同▲4％pt） 、非製造業で▲47％pt（同▲1％pt）となった。 
先行きは、大企業製造業で▲ 27％pt（今回差▲5％pt）、大企業非製造業で▲ 37％pt（同±
0％pt）、中小企業製造業で▲37％pt（同▲5％pt）、中小企業非製造業で▲50％pt（同▲3％pt）
と見込まれている。中小企業を中心に雇用の不足感は一段と強まる見込みだ。

【企業金融】緩和的な金融環境はおおむね維持も、借入金利が上昇 
資金繰り判断 DI（最近）は、大企業全産業で＋13％pt（前回差＋1％pt） 、中小企業全産業で
＋8％pt（同±0％pt）となった。また、金融機関の貸出態度判断 DI（最近）は、大企業全産業
で＋12％pt（前回差▲1％pt） 、中小企業全産業で＋12％pt（同±0％pt）と、横ばい圏で推移
した。金融環境はおおむね緩和的な状態が維持されているといえる。 
他方、借入金利水準判断 DI（最近）は、大企業全産業で＋65％pt（前回差＋6％pt） 、中小企
業全産業で＋70％pt（同＋9％pt）と上昇した。先行きについても、大企業全産業 で＋67％pt
（今回差＋2％pt） 、中小企業全産業で＋74％pt（同＋4％pt）と一段の上昇が見込まれている。
資金繰りへの影響は現時点では限定的であるものの、利上げの進展を受けて、中小企業を中心
に資金調達コストの上昇圧力は今後も強まる見込みだ。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 30 日

1 群益投顧 
金像電 (2368 TT) 於 09/29/2026 董事會核准總金額 79 億 元 的 資 本 支 出 預 算 案 ， 預 計 自
4Q26 起 陸 續 進 行 投 資，並 決 議 洽 定 特 定 應 募 人 為Amazon.com, Inc(AMZN US) ，實 際
私募價格為 856 元 ， 預 計 此 次 私 募 規 模 為 15.89 億元、占金像電 2Q26 股本 0.36% 。
研 究 部 認 為，金 像 電 作 為 既 有 ASIC 伺 服 器 板 的 生 產 廠 商，隨 著 CSP 業者未來資本支
出 逐 步 放 大 、 對 算 力 的 要 求 也 直 接 提 升 PCB 板 規 格 與 產 品 種 類 ， 金 像 電 也 勢 必 增 加
資 本 支 出 以 維 持 產 業 競 爭 力 。 綜 合 以 上 ， 考 量 新 產 品 、 新 業 務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注 金
像電 2027 年的營運，故維持金像電 Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,480 元 (22.70 PER X 
2027F EPS) 。

研 究 員 ： 吳 建 弘   
A51102@cim.capital.com.tw

金 像 電 09/29 /2026 董 事 會 核 准 資 本 支 出 案 與 公 告 第 一 次 私 募 案 對 象：  
金像電 (2368 TT) 於 09/29/2026 董 事 會 核 准 總 金 額 79 億 元 的 資 本 支 出 預 算
案 ， 預 計 自 4Q26 起 陸 續 進 行 投 資 ， 主 要 進 行 38 億 元 的 土 地 預 算 、41 億元
的 建 廠 與 設 備 資 本 支 出 。 
並決議 2026 年 第 一 次 私 募 普 通 股 定 價 及 應 募 人 資 訊 ，已 洽 定 特 定 應 募 人 為
Amazon.com, Inc(AMZN US) ， 預 計 本 次 私 募 增 資 認 購 股 數 為 1,856,308 股，
實 際 私 募 價 格為 856 元 。 此 次 私 募 規 模 為 15.89 億 元 、 占 金 像 電 2Q26 股本
0.36% 。  
研 究 部 評 析 
參考 04/16/2026 金 像 電 決 議 辦 理 私 募 增 資 案 的 內 容 ， 此 次Amazon 入股，
預 計 將 擴 大金 像 電 的 伺 服 器 業 務 範 圍 ， 提 高 營 運 績 效 。而 金 像 電 作 為既有
ASIC 伺 服 器 板 的 生 產 廠 商 ， 隨 著 CSP 業 者 未 來 資 本 支 出 逐 步 放 大 、 對 算
力 的 要 求 直 接 提 升 PCB 板 規 格 與 產 品 種 類 ， 金 像 電 也 勢 必 增 加 資 本 支 出 以
維 持 產 業 競 爭 力 。 
展望 2027 年 ， 隨 著 新世代的 AI ASIC 伺 服 器 產 品 逐 漸 量 產 ， 新 產 品 、 新
業務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注金 像 電 營 運 ， 推 升 後 續 展 望 。 而 09/29/2026 的資
本 支 出 案 ， 研 究 部 基 於 設 備 交 期 規 劃 ， 預 計 將 投 入2028 年後 的 營運。  
綜 合 以 上 ， 考 量新 產 品 、 新 業 務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注 金 像 電2027 年的營
運， 故 維 持 金 像 電 Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,480 元 (22.70 PER X 2027F 
EPS) 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IN FRANCE: DOUBLE
-
DIGIT GROWTH IN BUSINESS CREATIONS,
LOWER GROWTH IN BANKRUPTCIES,
AND STABLE NON
-
PERFORMING LOANS
ECO CHARTS | BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IS A NEW PUBLIC
ATION AIMED AT PROVIDING AN OVERVIEW OF THE DYNAMICS
OF BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IN FRANCE, AS WELL AS THE
RISKS ASSOCIATED WITH THE LATTER. WE WILL PUBLISH
REGULAR UPDATES TO THIS PUBLICATION ON A QUARTERLY BASIS.

digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in bankruptcies and stable non
-
performing loans

ECONOMIC STUDIES
•
Business
creations
rose
by
12
%
y/y
in
the
first
eight
months
of
2026
,
marking
an

compared
with
the
growth
seen
in
2025
(+
5
.
3
%
)
.
•
This
increase
was
driven
by
sectors
associated
with
new
information
and
communication
technologies,
where
the
trend
is
gaining
momentum
.
•
Meanwhile,
business
bankruptcies

rose
by
5
.
3
%
y/y
from

to
July,
according
to
the
data
from
the
Banque
de
France
(vs
+
3
.
6
%
y/y
in
2025
)
.
After
a

rise
in
the
first
quarter
(+
8
%
y/y),
this
increase
has
since
slowed
.
This

could
continue,
as,

to
Altares,
payment

have
decreased
to

12
.
3

of
2026
(
14
.
1
days

earlier),
compared
with
the
European
average
of
13
.
1
days
.
•
The

rise
in
business
bankruptcies
is
most
pronounced
in

services
sector,
which
has
simultaneously
seen
an

in
business

.

2026
,

are
up
3
%
.
•

since

2026
.

Sources

5
Variable
Source
Frequency
Definition
Business bankruptcies
Banque de France
Monthly
Companies subject to collective proceedings (receivership and judicial liquidation). Monthly data from the
Banque de France aggregated over a quarter to minimise the risk of calendar effects
Business creations
Insee
Monthly
Survey of business creations (all types, companies, sole traders, micro-entrepreneurs). Monthly data from
INSEE aggregated over a quarter to minimise the risk of calendar effects

Non performing loans ratio
EBA
Quarterly
Gross non performing loans and advances at amortised cost / Total gross loans and advances at
amortised cost
Share of underperforming loans (Stage 2 IFRS 9)
BCE
Quarterly
Loans and advances in Stage 2 / Total loans and advances. Loans and advances at amortised cost and at
fair value through equity
Economic weight of bankruptcies
Banque de France
Monthly
Total outstanding bank loans to newly bankrupt NFCs over a one-year period, calculated as the figure for

the month in which the firm is declared bankrupt, relative to the average outstanding bank loans to both
healthy and bankrupt NFCs during the same period.
Share of 90+ days past due payments
URSSAF
Monthly
Contributions still to be collected by Urssaf / Amount of contributions due to Urssaf

## Document 209: CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn(SCS) 
Thách thức từ Long Thành 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS từ 2H2026

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản lượng 
và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, triển vọng 
dài hạn phụ thuộc khả năng tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga mới

Cổ tức 2026 dự kiến ở mức 5,000 
VND/cổ phiếu

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang svck). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 tỷ 
VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự suy 
giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng mùa cao điểm hàng hóa cuối năm và mạng bay Trung Đông 
phục hồi sẽ hỗ trợ sản lượng SCS trong những tháng cuối năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ 
cải thiện còn hạn chế do nhu cầu vận tải trên các tuyến Trung Đông – châu Á và 
châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn suy yếu. Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự 
kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy).

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sau khi các hạ tầng kết nối với sân bay Long Thành được hoàn 
thiện, tỷ lệ phân bổ chuyến bay quốc tế giữa Long Thành và Tân Sơn Nhất sẽ dao 
động quanh khoảng 75%/25%. Tỷ trọng phân bổ hàng quốc tế tại TP. Hồ Chí Minh 
qua Long Thành 2027/2028/2029 dự kiến sẽ đạt 10%/35%/55%.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì cổ tức tiền mặt cao nhờ khả năng tạo dòng 
tiền ổn định và quy mô tiền gửi lớn. Cổ tức năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ 
phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027 nhằm tích lũy 
nguồn lực cho các dự án mới.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh và kết quả định giá, chúng tôi đưa ra khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP với cổ phiếu SCS, mức giá mục tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu.

Trung lập duy trì 
Giá mục tiêu VND 50,700 
Tăng/giảm (%) 14.3% 
Giá hiện tại (22/09/2026) VND 44,350 
Giá mục tiêu đồng thuận VND 56,900 
Vốn hóa thị trường  
(nghìn tỷ VND/ tỷ USD) 
4.3/0.2

### Page 2

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ hàng hóa Sài Gòn được thành 
lập năm 2008, hiện là đơn vị khai thác nhà ga 
hàng hóa hàng không lớn thứ 2 tại cảng 
hàng không quốc tế (HKQT) Tân Sơn Nhất 
với thị phần khoảng 50%. Các cổ đông lớn 
của SCS bao gồm CTCP Gemadept (33.4%) 
và Tổng Công ty Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam 
(13.6%). 
Hoạt động khai thác nhà ga là mảng kinh 
doanh chính của SCS. Hiện SCS đang tiếp 
tục mua sắm trang thiết bị nâng công suất 
xử lý hàng lên 350,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy) dù đã được hỗ 
trợ bởi mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi và mùa cao điểm cuối năm 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Thông lượng hàng hóa quốc tế qua SCS bắt đầu giảm từ 2027 với 10%/35%/55%/70% hàng quốc tế 
Tân Sơn Nhất phân bổ sang cho L TA vào 2027/2028/2029/2030, trước khi bắt đầu tăng trưởng trở lại từ 
2031. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định nhờ nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh, cổ tức 2026 
dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu và duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang yoy). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 
tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự 
suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Biểu đồ 4. Cơ cấu thông lượng hàng SCS (tấn)  Biểu đồ 5. Giá dịch vụ và biên LNG hoạt động khai thác nhà ga 
của SCS

Nguồn: SCS  Nguồn: SCS

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS trong 2H2026, 
song mức độ cải thiện còn hạn chế 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng hàng hóa của SCS sẽ cải thiện trong những tháng 
cuối năm 2026 nhờ mạng bay Trung Đông dần được khôi phục, đặc biệt từ 
Qatar Airways, cùng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa tăng trong mùa cao điểm cuối 
năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ phục hồi dự kiến còn hạn chế khi nhu cầu vận tải trên 
các tuyến Trung Đông – Châu Á và Châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn giảm lần lượt 
14.1% và 16.1% yoy trong tháng 7/2026 (tháng thứ 5 liên tiếp), trái ngược với 
mức tăng 3.9% của thị trường hàng hóa hàng không toàn cầu. Diễn biến này 
phần nào giải thích việc sản lượng hàng quốc tế của SCS tiếp tục giảm khoảng 
10% yoy trong tháng 7–8, dù tần suất bay của các hãng Trung Đông đã phục 
hồi đáng kể.

Chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng hàng quốc tế và nội địa trong bốn tháng cuối 
năm lần lượt giảm 4% và 2% yoy, cả năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy) và 
52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy). Doanh thu khai thác nhà ga được kỳ vọng tiếp tục 
được hỗ trợ bởi việc điều chỉnh tăng giá dịch vụ từ đầu năm, bù đắp một phần 
tác động từ sản lượng suy giảm.

Biểu đồ 6. Mức độ phục hồi chuyến bay của các hãng hàng không vùng Vịnh 
Đến ngày 21/09/2026, tần suất bay của các 
hãng vùng Vịnh đã phục hồi đáng kể, phần 
lớn đạt khoảng 85–100% mức trước xung đột, 
riêng Qatar Airways đạt 97%, tăng từ mức 
87% ghi nhận giữa tháng 6.

### Page 5

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản 
lượng và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, 
triển vọng dài hạn phụ thuộc khả 
năng tham gia vận hành nhà ga mới 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa hàng không khu vực phía Nam duy 
trì tăng trưởng trong trung hạn, với sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa tăng 8%/4% 
yoy năm 2027 và 9%/6% mỗi năm từ 2028, được hỗ trợ bởi hoạt động xuất 
nhập khẩu, thương mại điện tử và sự phát triển của chuỗi cung ứng. Tuy nhiên, 
sản lượng hàng quốc tế qua SCS dự kiến chịu áp lực khi các chuyến bay từng 
bước chuyển sang Long Thành. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi giả định tỷ lệ 
chuyến bay quốc tế chuyển sang Long Thành lần lượt đạt 10%/35%/55%/70% 
trong giai đoạn 2027–2030, với tác động lên sản lượng thực tế phụ thuộc vào 
tiến độ khai thác nhà ga hàng hóa và hệ thống logistics tại sân bay mới.

Đồng thời, giá dịch vụ quốc tế dự kiến bắt đầu giảm từ giữa năm 2027 nhằm giữ 
chân các hãng hàng không, trong khi giá nội địa kỳ vọng duy trì tăng 3%/năm. 
Theo đó, biên lợi nhuận gộp dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong năm 2027 và chịu áp lực 
đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ trọng hàng quốc tế có mức giá dịch vụ cao suy giảm.

Bên cạnh đó, SCS có cơ hội mở rộng quy mô khai thác trong dài hạn thông qua 
việc tham gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành, công suất thiết kế 
550 nghìn tấn/năm. Doanh nghiệp đã ký biên bản ghi nhớ với ACV về khả năng 
tham gia vận hành nhà ga này, qua đó có thể tiếp cận nguồn hàng dịch chuyển 
từ Tân Sơn Nhất nếu được lựa chọn. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu 
từ dự án vào kịch bản cơ sở khi chưa có kết quả lựa chọn đơn vị vận hành chính 
thức.

Biểu đồ 7. Thông lượng hàng và biên LNG của SCS 2022 – 2028F

Nguồn: SCS, KBSV dự phóng

Nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh và 
chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định 
hỗ trợ định giá cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định trong 
những năm tới nhờ khả năng tạo dòng tiền cao, quy mô tiền gửi lớn và mức 
đòn bẩy tài chính thấp. Trong 1H2026, thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh đã bù đắp 
phần lớn sự suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga, giúp LNST duy 
trì ổn định ở mức 361 tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy). Chúng tôi dự phóng cổ tức tiền mặt 
năm 2026 tiếp tục đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 
VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027, phù hợp với triển vọng lợi nhuận chịu áp lực từ 
Long Thành và nhu cầu tích lũy nguồn lực tài chính để tham gia các dự án mới.

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu  
 Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền (DCF), trên cơ sở cập nhật triển 
vọng kinh doanh và tác động từ quá trình chuyển đổi khai thác sang Long 
Thành, chúng tôi đưa ra giá mục tiêu cho SCS là 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương với mức tiềm năng sinh lời 14.3% so với mức giá đóng cửa ngày 
22/09/2026. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu từ việc tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành vào định giá. Các giả định 
chính bao gồm: 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc tế năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 4% yoy do mạng bay Trung Đông chưa phục 
hồi hoàn toàn. Nhu cầu hàng quốc tế khu vực phía nam dự kiến tăng 
8% năm 2027 và 9%/năm trong giai đoạn 2028–2030, kéo theo lượng 
hàng qua SCS trong 2027/2028/2029/2030 sẽ giảm lần lượt -2.8%/-
21%/-25%/-27% yoy, trước khi tăng trưởng trở lại từ 2031 sau khi đạt 
tỷ trọng phân bổ tối ưu giữa SGN và LTA. 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc nội năm 2026 đạt 52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 2% yoy do áp lực chi phí vận tải và điều 
chỉnh mạng bay. Sản lượng dự kiến phục hồi 4% năm 2027 và 6%/năm 
từ 2028 khi chi phí nhiên liệu hạ nhiệt và năng lực khai thác cải thiện. 
- Giá nội địa dự kiến tăng 3%/năm, trong khi giá quốc tế bắt đầu giảm 
từ giữa 2027 nhằm giữ chân các hãng hàng không. Biên lợi nhuận gộp 
dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong 2027 và chịu áp lực đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ 
trọng hàng quốc tế suy giảm và chi phí cố định không giảm tương ứng

## Document 226: EMEA FX Talking_ External headwinds up against local stories

Source PDF: all report pdf/EMEA FX Talking_ External headwinds up against local stories.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Like many emerging market currencies, those in the EMEA
region have been hit by higher US Treasury yields and the
stronger dollar. Yet the domestic stories look constructive for
many, and a combination of some local rate hikes as well as
light foreign positioning (ex Hungary) can insulate these
currencies from the global bond market sell-oﬀ

Rafal Benecki, Peter Virovacz, David Havrlant, Mateusz Sutowicz,
Dmitry Dolgin, Muhammet Mercan and Chris Turner
Light foreign positioning and some local rate hikes can keep EMEA currencies
relatively insulated
EUR/PLN: Zloty swings with market sentiment
  Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 12M
EUR/PLN 4.3877 Mildly Bearish 4.36 4.35 4.33 4.29
EUR/PLN has moved towards 4.40 after trading around the 4.30
level during summer. With market sentiment deteriorating amid
geopolitical tensions in Iran, France and Russia, the coming weeks
could challenge the zloty.

### Page 2

PLN has recently strengthened its correlation with EUR/USD, so the
dollar’s future moves will be the key driver in the currency’s
performance, while domestic factors are likely to take a back seat.
We retain a broadly positive stance on the zloty in the medium
term due to solid GDP momentum and persistent outperformance.
However, the balance of risks has become less favourable; the
National Bank of Poland may deliver fewer hikes than priced, and a

hot budget debate could reveal vulnerabilities.
Source: Reﬁnitiv, ING Forecasts
EUR/HUF: A credibility boost is in the making for
the forint
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 1
EUR/HUF 364.89 Bearish 360.00 355.00 360.00 36
While EUR/HUF has shown some volatility recently, the swings have
been far more moderate than we used to see. This reﬂects general
positioning and the euro adoption story.
We think an idiosyncratic shock is coming with the 2027 budget

and the mid-term ﬁscal consolidation plan. If the government
delivers a credible plan, as we expect, and announces oﬃcial talks
on joining ERM II, this could provide a credibility boost to the forint.
A signiﬁcant drop in the risk premium and some insulation from
geopolitical market turbulence could take EUR/HUF down to the
350-355 area over the next month. This would, however, reopen
the door to a National Bank of Hungary easing cycle starting in

December, putting the HUF on a mild depreciation path over the
following 12 months.

EUR/CZK: Braving the turbulence
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 12M
EUR/CZK 24.39 Neutral 24.40 24.40 24.35 24.30
The Czech Republic has also been aﬀected by the global bond yield
rally, with the turmoil applying depreciation pressure on the
koruna. Inﬂation is set to increase on the back of rallying import
prices and second-round eﬀects of the oil price shock starting to
bite.
We believe that equilibrium interest rates are higher than generally

assumed, since the cost of capital must adjust to a world of
protracted conﬂict and increased resource scarcity. A higher base
rate is warranted to prevent the koruna from weakening further,
which would exacerbate domestic price pressures.
That said, the ECB may turn more hesitant despite elevated
inﬂation, as concerns over French ﬁscal issues rekindle fears of
another euro crisis. In turn, the real interest rate diﬀerential should
help the koruna withstand the turmoil and gradually appreciate

once things have calmed down.

EUR/RON: Rating downgrade avoided, RON still
vulnerable
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 12M
EUR/RON 5.3383 Neutral 5.35 5.35 5.40 5.45
EUR/RON had its second major move of the year, climbing from
5.28 to 5.35, which looks to be the near-term level. Oﬃcial
interventions have likely moderated the impact of signiﬁcant
outﬂow pressures through both September and so far in October –
with the fear of rating downgrades and the protracted political

instability as key drivers.
While the rating remained in place, the political arena is yet to ﬁnd
its consensus. The key positive element supporting the credit story
remains the budget deﬁcit, which stood at 2.89% of GDP as of
August 2026 (vs 4.50% in August last year).
A tighter liquidity management has recently led FX implied yields
to the highest levels since May 2025, although funding pressures
have diminished from their early October peak. We expect the pair

to remain around its current levels by year-end and close next year
near 5.45.

EUR/RSD: RSD stable ahead of parliamentary
elections
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 1
EUR/RSD 117.42 Neutral 117.40 117.40 117.30 11
EUR/RSD has remained relatively ﬂat in the range of 117.3–117.5.
Yet again, the US Treasury's Oﬃce of Foreign Assets Control (OFAC)
has extended the operating waiver for Serbia's NIS reﬁnery until 30
October. The NIS ownership issue is yet to be resolved.
Snap parliamentary elections have been called for 25 October, with

President Aleksandar Vucic resigning in order to pursue
premiership. The ﬁscal deﬁcit has recently been revised higher,
from 3.0% to 3.5% of GDP in 2026, mainly reﬂecting higher social
expenditure.
At its October meeting, the National Bank of Serbia kept the key
rate in place at 5.75%, continuing to highlight inﬂation uncertainty.
Between January and August, the Bank sold €407m to keep the
pair stable. We expect the pair to remain around the current levels

ahead.

USD/UAH: NBU efforts to protect the UAH
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 12M
USD/UAH 44.92 Neutral 45.00 45.20 45.30 45.50
The hryvnia remained under pressure as the dollar strengthened,
partly due to renewed Middle East tensions. Consequently,
USD/UAH temporarily broke above 45.0, reaching its highest level
on record.
Hryvnia depreciation could have been sharper, but National Bank of
Ukraine action provided support. In September, the central bank

raised rates by 50bp to 16.0% and maintained sizeable FX
interventions, with the four-week average near record highs.
The outlook for the hryvnia appears cloudy as the macroeconomic
environment remains challenging – particularly as the ongoing war
with Russia poses an increasing threat to the Ukrainian economy.

USD/KZT: Growing state FX sales backlog to
boost KZT resilience
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 1
USD/KZT 452.04 Neutral 455.00 450.00 440.00 46
The tenge gained 5% against the US dollar in September, driven
mainly by private capital inﬂows, particularly continued non-
resident demand for KZT government bonds.
Combined net FX sales by the state oil fund (NFRK) and National
Bank of Kazakhstan fell to zero in September, compared with $1.0–

1.3bn per month in 1H26. As NFRK sales have remained below ﬁscal
ﬁnancing needs since mid-2026, an estimated $1.0–1.5bn backlog
of FX sales has accumulated.
After gaining 25% YoY against USD, the tenge has limited upside,
particularly amid global risk aversion and regional geopolitical
uncertainty. At the same time, Kazakhstan’s high real rates should
continue to attract carry-trade inﬂows, while the eventual release
of the state FX sales backlog could ensure KZT resilience if market

sentiment deteriorates.

USD/UZS: Still waiting for gold exports to catch
up
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6
USD/UZS 11898.00 Bearish 11650.00 11500.00 1160
After appreciating 1% against the US dollar in August, the soum
was broadly ﬂat in September and early October. This was slightly
weaker than we expected, but still resilient given the global dollar
rally and lower gold prices.
Gold exports remain a key variable for Uzbekistan’s FX market.

There were no exports in August, and gold was exported only in
April and July this year. Year-to-date volumes stand at just 18 mt,
implying a substantial backlog compared with 85 mt in FY25 and
the historical annual range of 80–120 mt.
Like Kazakhstan, Uzbekistan oﬀers high real rates and is likely to
continue doing so despite a potential monetary policy easing. As
inﬂation has stabilised at 6.2–6.4% YoY, we see some room for a
cautious cut from the current 14.00% policy rate before the end of

the year.

USD/TRY: Rate cut expectations build as
inﬂation eases
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 12M
USD/TRY 49.34 Bullish 49.75 51.50 54.30 59.80
Fund volatility has not aﬀected the dollarisation ratio. Net outﬂows
in lira-denominated funds turned TRY625bn between the end of
August and 6 October, excluding suspended funds. Meanwhile,
outﬂows from FX-linked fund assets totalled $2.2bn, while higher
TRY deposits and repo balances suggest dollarisation remains

contained.
September’s CPI came in below consensus, supported by
favourable food and transport. Annual CPI fell to 29.7% from 31.5%
in August. Seasonally adjusted CPI, a key indicator closely
monitored by the Central Bank of Turkey, slowed to 1.6% month-
on-month from 2.2% in August, marking one of the lowest readings
seen in recent years.
This backdrop increases the likelihood of a shift toward policy rate
cuts. The recent investment fund disruption is likely to raise

downside risks to activity. Given September’s CPI and weak
domestic demand, we expect two 100bp cuts in October and
December.

USD/ZAR: A resilient performance from the rand
Spot One month bias 1M 3M 6M 1
USD/ZAR 16.51 Mildly Bearish 16.50 16.50 16.25 16
The high-yield rand has been one of the better EM performers
during the sell-oﬀ over the last month. Its ﬁscal metrics are not
particularly great, (5% budget deﬁcit, 80% debt to GDP), but the
coalition government looks to be taking things in the right
direction; a primary budget surplus is helpful here.

The South African Reserve Bank is helping out too, having hiked
25bp in September to 7.25%. It is threatening to hike rates again
on a baseline view that core central banks hike another 50bp.
A diﬃcult external environment suggests USD/ZAR may need to
trade in a 16.00-16.60 range for the next three to six months. As
2027 progresses, however, the case for sub-16.00 levels builds.

Israel’s high-quality credit rating (A-) has provided a buﬀer to the
shekel during this debt crisis. That largely stems from Israel’s strong
external investment position and relatively large domestic capital
market. Additionally, the debt sell-oﬀ has yet to unhinge the US
tech story – of which the shekel is a beneﬁciary.

In September, the Bank of Israel cut rates to 3.25%, citing low
inﬂation. Markets currently price further aggressive cuts to the
2.00% area, which seems excessive. The BoI might prefer to save
those rate cuts for when the dollar is broadly softer – maybe in
2Q27 – and when USD/ILS is threatening to run away sub-3.00.
Longer term, the trade-weighted ILS is very ﬁrm, and we suspect
Israeli authorities will struggle to tolerate much further strength.

## Document 287: new era of chip testing 20261008 ubs

Source PDF: all report pdf/new era of chip testing 20261008 ubs.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 31
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 8 October 2026
Global Research
Asia Semiconductor Insight
AI put to the test: the new era of chip testing
Time to revisit semiconductor testing as content growth accelerates
Our core thesis is that AI-driven chip complexity is drastically increasing testing content
across wafer, package, and system levels, creating a multi-year growth opportunity that
the market has yet to fully price in. Testing companies in Taiwan have underperformed
TAIEX in the past three months (indexing at -9% vs TAIEX +7%), yet to recover from the

July market deleveraging. Investor hesitation currently stems from 1) fear of test content
growth expectations already priced-in, 2) lingering concerns over shorter test times, and
3) near-term capacity constraints among major suppliers of probe cards, load boards,
socket pins, testers, and handlers, caused by heightened testing demand. Ultimately,
these concerns overshadow the structural increase in test insertions required by AI
accelerators, chiplets, and advanced packaging. As advanced packaging scales, testing

intensity must rise in tandem, driving growth through higher content per device rather
than semiconductor unit growth alone.
Expanding testing opportunities with shift-left for CP and increasing FT content
Chip probing (CP) supply chain should be the key beneficiary of the industry's shift-left
testing trend, as more test insertions move to the wafer level before packaging, in
addition to longer CP time. We believe investors underestimate this trend, which should

accelerate with broader adoption of 2.5D/3D packaging, chiplets and larger AI
packages, driving demand for high pin count probe cards, probers, and thermal-control
equipment. We expect strong growth for Hermes Testing (HTSI)  as thermal-
management requirements rise with increasing chip power and complexity, with prober
thermal-control module shipments reaching 3,000/4,100 units in 2027E/28E, up from
1,200 in 2026E . We forecast Chunghwa Precision Test (CHPT) 's sales to exceed

consensus by 7%/15% in 2027E/28E, supported by structural AI/HPC testing demand
and rising value-added content. Importantly, shift-left testing does not diminish the role
of final test (FT) and mission-mode testing. Test content and test time should continue to
increase as validation becomes increasingly critical for complex, large-form-factor AI
accelerators. We expect larger package sizes and rising system-level testing
requirements to drive demand for WinWay's high-pin-count sockets, supporting

74%/71% YoY sales growth in 2027E/28E.
Future of testing: co-packaged optics (CPO) will be a major opportunity
CPO testing represents a significant new market opportunity, though we expect the
ecosystem to remain fragmented rather than consolidating around a single winner. As AI
scaling shifts the industry bottleneck from memory to interconnect, CPO adoption is
becoming increasingly important, creating new testing challenges that will require
collaboration across equipment, optics, and test solution providers. Leveraging

ficonTEC's optical alignment expertise and HTSI's probe, testing, and manufacturing
capabilities, we estimate the HTSI  will deliver 40 units of insertion 2 equipment and 70
units of insertion 3 testing equipment in 2027 , with further upside in 2028 as CPO
deployment expands from scale-out towards scale-up.
Initiate Hermes Testing, Chunghwa Precision Test, WinWay with Buy ratings
We initiate coverage on semiconductor testing solution provider Hermes Testing with

a Buy rating,  NT$8,880 price target based on 31x 2028E PE, for thermal control/CPO
with the most attractive risk/reward in our testing coverage; test interface PCB supplier
CHPT with a Buy rating, NT$5,000 price target based on 31x avg 2027-28E PE, for
CPU/TPU programs and test board content gain; and semiconductor test socket leader
WinWay with a Buy rating, NT$8,900 price target based on 30x avg 2027-28E PE,
for socket content growth and margin recovery. Among our testing coverage, we also

including information on the Quantitative Research Review published by UBS, begin on page 22.

Asia Semiconductor Insight UBS Research
Sector Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP MOST FAVORED
HTSI, CHPT, WinWay, Hon Precision, Chroma ATE
LEAST FAVORED
N/A
Q: Is chip probing becoming increasingly important in the testing supply chain?
Yes.   As package costs rise, foundries must improve known-good-die (KGD) screening before
packaging, increasing demand for wafer-level testing. This should drive demand for probe cards,

probers and advanced thermal control solutions capable of managing higher power consumption,
wafer warpage and tighter test tolerances. We forecast HTSI/CHPT/WinWay' probe card business to
see a 31%/35%/33% sales CAGR for 2026-2031E, as they are positioned to grow strongly given
their smaller scale and robust growth drivers from shift left, CPU, AI accelerators. We forecast HTSI to
ship 1,200/3,000/4,100 thermal control modules in 2026E/27E/28E, as advanced wafer-level test for

AI accelerators will require thermal control solutions in order to prevent probe card needle burnt.
Q: Will FT and SLT content continue to grow in the coming years?
Yes. Despite concerns that final-test (FT) time could flatten or decline with Nvidia's Rubin GPUs, we
expect overall FT content to continue growing as advanced packaging complexity and chip sizes
increase. We also expect broader adoption of system-level testing (SLT)/mission-mode testing, as AI

accelerators require increasingly stringent validation to ensure optimal system and rack-level
performance. We forecast WinWay's socket business to see a 33% sales CAGR for 2026-2031E.
Q: Will CPO testing be a revenue generator in 2027-28 to supply chain players?
Yes. Testing remains a key bottleneck, creating a new equipment opportunity beginning in 2027E.
We expect initial CPO volumes in 2027, followed by broader adoption in 2028. Testing remains one

of the key bottlenecks to commercialization, driving demand for new equipment and test solutions.
We believe early leaders such as ficonTEC (partnership with HTSI) are well positioned to benefit from
the initial ramp, alongside suppliers including Chroma ATE and Hon Precision.
1) We believe upside from agentic AI CPU demand is not reflected in CHPT and WinWay shares. We
estimate CPU-related demand could contribute 20-30% of 2027E sales  for CHPT and WinWay,

respectively, with further upside in 2028E. 2) We believe market expectations for 2027 CPO testing
equipment shipments remain conservative, particularly for insertion 2 equipment. Based on our
industry analysis, we estimate HTSI will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, this
should be higher than the market's expectation of no insertion 2 and limited insertion 3 shipment
due to engineering bottleneck. We also see further upside in 2028E as CPO adoption expands.

Testing is becoming one of the largest incremental beneficiaries of AI-driven semiconductor
complexity. As more test insertions are required across wafer, package and system levels, testing
content per device should increase materially, expanding the industry's addressable market and
supporting multi-year growth across the supply chain.
TSMC and OSATs have been expanding their CoWoS capacities aggressively, from 40kwpm in 2024
to around 90 kwpm in 2025, and tracking to expand CoWoS capacity toward 160kwpm  in 2026E.

TSMC also ramped up expanding N3/N2 capacity, with multiple product launches from customers'
pipelines—Nvidia's Rubin, AMD's MI400 series/Venice CPU and other cloud ASICs—translating into
foreseeable, strong testing demand.

Source: UBS estimates
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing
With the industry increasingly adopting shift-left testing, chip probing (CP) and its supply
chain should be key beneficiaries of a multi-year structural growth trend. This underpins

our above-consensus forecasts for Chunghwa Precision Test (CHPT), with 2027E/28E
sales 7%/15% above consensus driven by stronger AI/HPC demand, capacity expansion,
and rising value-added content. WinWay should also benefit through its partnership
with Technoprobe, with probe card revenue growth remaining robust at 94%/72% in
2026E/27E following 161%/260% growth in 2024/25.
Hermes Testing (HTSI) should be another major beneficiary as increasing chip power

and complexity drive demand for advanced thermal-management modules used
alongside high-end probing systems. With TEL's leading AI-oriented probers typically
paired with advanced thermal-control solutions, we expect HTSI's thermal-module
shipments to reach 3,000 units in 2027E from 1,200 in 2026E. Combined with testing,
installation and service revenue, this should drive HTSI sales growth of 127%/152% in
2026E/27E.
Accelerators still in focus with TPU as another significant growth driver

Nvidia remains a key growth driver for both CHPT and WinWay. We expect both
companies to gain share with Rubin, with WinWay's socket share improving from
roughly 50% historically to 60% with Rubin, while CHPT is positioned to supply around
70% of probe card and final test PCB content. Nvidia currently accounts for roughly
50% of WinWay's revenue and 30% of CHPT's.
Beyond Nvidia, we view Google's TPU roadmap as a meaningful incremental
opportunity. Following the successful ramp of TPU v8t with MediaTek as design partner,

CHPT is positioned to supply probe card PCBs, final test load boards, system-level test
boards and burn-in boards. WinWay will supply sockets for both TPU v8t and v9, with
the larger v9 package providing another growth leg. We estimate production of 4mn
TPU v8t units in 2027E and 2.3mn TPU v9 packages (9.2mn dies) alongside 2m v8t units
in 2028E.
CPU business as a supplemental catalyst for test interface in an agentic
world
With agentic AI proliferation, CPU demand is increasing significantly and this directly

correlates to increasing demand for testing consumables. WinWay and CHPT should
both benefit from the strong ramp up of Nvidia's ARM based Vera CPU and Google's
ARM based Axion CPU in 2026E and 2027E. WinWay will be supplying sockets and
probe cards for Vera and Axion, and CHPT will be the probe card and PCB supplier for
both Vera and Axion. WinWay will have the additional upside from AMD's x86 Venice
CPU, and CHPT could capture some share through AMD's equipment vendor

procurement. We estimate CHPT's CPU related sales to be low to mid-teens % of total
sales in 2026E and growing to 20-30% in 2027E  with CHPT's share expansions with
turnkey probe card solutions for Google's Axion. For WinWay, we believe CPU should
account for around 30% of sales in 2026E/27E.

### Page 4

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 4
CPO testing fuelling future growth for semiconductor testing
ficonTEC's 25 years of precision optical-test expertise makes it an early leader in CPO test
equipment, particularly in optical alignment, a critical capability for high-volume
manufacturing. Through its partnership with ficonTEC, HTSI is well positioned to
benefit from the initial CPO ramp in 2027E-28E by supporting manufacturing scale-up.

We expect both insertion 2 and insertion 3 testing to be required over the next two
years. Despite the technical challenges of double-sided wafer probing, insertion 2
testing remains important given current optical-engine yields and the need to identify
known-good optical engines before fibre-array-unit (FAU) coupling. This becomes
increasingly critical as switch designs integrate larger numbers of optical engines.
We estimate HTSI will ship 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E , with

further upside in 2028E as CPO adoption expands and next-generation platforms move
into volume production.
Testing TAM expanding with IC complexity: leaders likely to
outperform industry growth
Our central view is that testing content per AI accelerator is growing faster than
semiconductor unit volumes, creating a multi-year expansion in testing TAM. We believe
semiconductor testing is entering a period of structural growth as AI-driven chip
complexity increases testing intensity across wafer, package and system levels. The

industry's shift-left strategy, which moves more testing upstream in the semiconductor
manufacturing process, is resulting in longer and more frequent test insertions. As cloud
AI GPUs and ASICs become increasingly complex, additional testing is required to ensure
functionality, performance and yield, while reducing defective parts per million (DPPM).
More robust testing should improve deployment efficiency and lower the cost of
operating AI infrastructure.

Semiconductor testing generally comprises four stages: wafer/chip probing (CP), final
test (FT), system-level test (SLT), and burn-in. CP is conducted at the wafer-level before
packaging, while FT, SLT, and burn-in occur after packaging. The below Figure 3S e m i c o n d u c t o r  t e s t i n g  p r o c e s s :  f r o m  w a f e r  l e v e l  t o  p a c k a g e  l e v e l
illustrates the semiconductor manufacturing flow.
Figure 3: Semiconductor testing process: from wafer level to package level

Wafer Manufacturing Wafer Probing Packaging Testing

Source: UBS
We estimate semiconductor testing historically accounts for ~1% of total
semiconductor revenue. Within testing, consumables such as probe cards, sockets and
interface boards represent ~10-15% of industry revenue. According to Yole, the probe
card, socket and interface board markets should grow from US$3.8bn, US$2.3bn and
US$1.7bn in 2026 to US$5.5bn, US$3.1bn and US$2.0bn by 2031, respectively.

### Page 5

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 5
Nonetheless, leaders within the testing sector are likely to stand out among competition
with greater sales CAGR comparing to the industry. Below we highlight Hermes
Testing (HTSI), Chunghwa Precision Test (CHPT), and WinWay's impressive growth
drivers and how this compares with broader industry sales CAGR.
We see HTSI's sales for the next few years to mainly  be driven by 1) thermal control
module shipment and 2) CPO equipment shipment. In the past, thermal control

modules weren't necessary for chip probing, due to less TDP for the chips. However, as
new iterations of AI accelerators now have substantial power draw, this unlocks a brand
new TAM for HTSI to enter with their thermal control modules to pair with probers, in
order to prevent probe card damage.
 Robust thermal control module shipments.  We estimate HTSI to ship
3,000/4,100 thermal control modules in 2027E/28E, with continuous demand in
future years,  bringing their 2026-31E semiconductor equipment sales CAGR to

48%.
 Thermal control module ASP expansion.  Gen 2 thermal control modules
should start to ship in 2027E which will drive tool ASP up around 25-50%.
 Demand supported by Advantest expansions. We believe the 3,000 thermal
control modules shipment forecast for 2027E is not far-fetched, as we read-across
from Advantest's announced capacity expansion plan (testers will be paired with
probers with a 1:1 ratio) with increasing capacity up to 5,000 testers within

FY2027/3 and they also announced a 10,000-unit target within CY2028.
Although the testers capacity expansion plan includes testers for final test, we do
see a good portion to be allocated to chip probing given the shift-left testing trend
increasing both testing insertions and testing time before chip packaging.
 CPO is another brand new TAM for semiconductor testing.  As insertion 2
and 3 testing equipment demand starts to rise in 2027E with scale-out CPO

adoption, we expect HTSI to ship 40/70 insertion 2 and 3 equipment in 2027E ,
with this number growing in 2028E to 80/160 and higher in future years as CPO
proliferates, and driving 52% 2026-31E test equipment & engineering services
sales CAGR.
 CPO to see content increase. Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment may start to ship in
2027E, with higher throughput and tool ASP .
 Insertion 2 might scale down, but not likely  any time soon.  Insertion 2
equipment shipment might come down in 2029E and beyond, as foundries scale-

up yield for photonic IC (PIC) in order to lessen the time consuming insertion 2
testing burden.

Uniquely positioned as a company with vertical integration capabilities with MEMS
probes, multi-layer PCB capabilities, and automation equipment all-in-house,  CHPT is
set to capture the growing test shift-left trend in the coming years, increasing both
testing insertions and testing time before chip packaging.
 CP to scale from a lower base. As a smaller probe card vendor, CHPT can benefit
from the growing demand for AI probe cards, with 2026-31E sales CAGR for

turnkey probe card robust at 41%.
 Also a FT/SLT/burn-in beneficiary. The company will also  see final test, system-
level test, burn-in content increase to play a role in their sales growth from their
IC Test Board sales CAGR of 39% in 2026-31E.
 Capacity expansion supporting growth.  As CHPT is currently capacity
constrained, the robust projected sales growth is enabled by CHPT's rapid capacity
expansion strategy with strong demand for its products, with one production

expansion scheduled for August 2026 and a second in March 2027, doubling and
tripling year-end 2025 capacity, respectively. Its Fab 3 will come online in Q428,
doubling existing capacity when fully ramped.
 MEMS capability to support share gain.  CHPT has MEMS probe capabilities
stemming from its history as a smartphone application processor (AP) probe card
supplier. It is in a good position to capture the growing demand for MEMS probe
cards as AI/HPC are shifting to MEMS probes.

 Probe card content expect to rise.  We also expect probe card content per
device to continue rising as AI accelerators grow in die size, pin count, power
consumption and packaging complexity. This should drive secular growth for both
probe card suppliers and upstream component providers, making chip probing
one of the most attractive areas within the semiconductor testing value chain.

As the leader in AI/HPC sockets, WinWay is set to deliver 2026-31E socket sales CAGR

The AI era has materially increased the value of semiconductor testing. Compared with
traditional mobile and automotive chips, AI accelerators incorporate larger compute
dies, more advanced nodes, chiplet architectures, advanced packaging technologies,
multiple I/O dies, and increasing HBM content. These factors drive higher-value test

consumables, including advanced MEMS probe cards, multilayer interface boards, and
next-generation sockets designed to support higher pin counts, thermal loading, and
power delivery requirements.
New iterations of semiconductors, especially for AI / HPC applications, are set to increase
in both front-end wafer fabrication advancements and back-end packaging
complexities with each iterations (i.e. Nvidia's roadmap from Blackwell to Rubin to
Feynman or Google's TPU roadmap from v7 to v8 to v9).   This trend is evident with

TSMC's front-end  and TSMC, Intel, and OSAT's back-end advanced packaging capacity
expansions. For front-end, we see industry N3 capacity expanding from 156kwpm in
year end 2025 to 224/283/293kpwm in year end 2026E/27E/28E, and N2 capacity from
47kwpm in year-end 2025 to 111/200/312kpwm in year end 2026E/27E/28E. For back-
end, we are seeing industry CoWoS capacity rising from 90kwpm in 2025 to 160kwpm
in 2026E and further expanding to 270kwpm in 2027E.

Final-test, system-level test, and burn-in content should continue to increase as AI
accelerators become larger, more power-hungry and more complex. Rising thermal

design power and packaging complexity increase validation requirements, driving
longer test times and higher demand for test equipment to prevent testing from
becoming a production bottleneck.

While architectural changes can affect test requirements for individual product
generations, we expect the long-term trend of rising testing content to remain intact.
For example, Nvidia's Rubin Ultra adopts a simpler package structure than previously
expected, from a 4-die structure to a 2-die structure and Rubin Ultra HBM de-spec from

HBM4E 12-Hi to initially use HBM4 8-Hi and upgrade to HBM4E 8-Hi at the latter stages,
which may moderate near-term test growth. However, future products such as Nvidia's
Feynman are expected to reintroduce greater package complexity, with a four-GPU per
package structure. More importantly, any reduction in test time can be offset by higher
production volumes, making throughput requirements equally important for testing
demand.

Below we summarize key technical items of probe cards, sockets, and load boards/PCB
used for semiconductor testing:
Probe cards and chip probing (CP)
Probe cards are used during chip probing (CP), the wafer-level stage of semiconductor

testing that identifies known-good dies (KGD) before packaging. As advanced
packaging technologies such as CoWoS, 3D stacking and hybrid bonding become more
prevalent, CP is becoming increasingly important because a single defective die or HBM
stack can compromise the entire package. This is driving the industry's shift-left testing
strategy, with more testing conducted earlier in the manufacturing process to identify
defects before costly packaging steps.

Probe cards consist of contact elements (probes) mounted on a PCB and are used
together with probers and automated test equipment (ATE). During testing, wafers are
placed on the prober chuck while the probe card makes electrical contact with individual
dies. The tester then performs electrical, parametric, and functional tests to determine
whether each die meets performance requirements. Dies that fail testing are removed
from the production flow prior to dicing and packaging.

Furthermore, probe cards are highly customised products designed for specific chip
architectures and testing platforms. As AI accelerators adopt larger dies, higher pin
counts and more advanced packaging technologies, probe card complexity and content
value continue to increase.

### Page 9

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 9
Figure 12: Chip probing utilise probe
cards to find known-good dies (KGD) and
eliminate faulty dies
Chip/Wafer Probing

The global probe card market remains concentrated among a handful of suppliers:
 FormFactor (FORM.O): World's largest probe card company (including both logic
and memory probe cards) by revenue as of year end 2025. Key supplier for HBM

probe cards.
 Technoprobe (TPRO.MI): World's largest logic probe card company by revenue
as of year end 2025. Key supplier for AI accelerators MEMS probe cards, especially
for GPU.
 MJC (6871.T): Japanese company, mainly specializing in memory probe cards, 3rd
largest probe card company by revenue as of year end 2025.
 MPI (6223.TWO) : Traditionally specializes in cantilever  probe cards, now
expanding its MEMS probe capacity. 4th largest probe card company by revenue as

of year end 2025. A probe card supplier of AI ASICs.
 JEM (6855.T): Mainly specialize in memory probe cards, 5th largest probe card
company by revenue as of year end 2025.
 WinWay (6515.TWO): One of the fastest-growing logic probe card suppliers.
Following its collaboration with Technoprobe, WinWay rose from the 15th-largest
probe card supplier in 2024 to the fifth-largest logic probe card supplier in 2025.
Probe card revenue increased from 11% of sales in 2024 to 29% in 2025.

 Chunghwa Precision Test Tech (6510.TW) : A key supplier for smartphone
application processor (AP) probe cards, and has been migrating to HPC related
applications in recent years. Although its share within the probe card market is
minor, it is a major supplier for probe card PCB, especially for high end probe cards
with advanced PCB that has high layer counts (>40 layers, and over 80 layers for AI
applications). Its key competitive advantage is its vertical integration as it

manufacturers the MEMS probe and PCB in-house.

Probe cards generally employ either vertical or cantilever architectures. Vertical probe
cards use probes arranged perpendicular to the substrate, enabling higher pin density

### Page 10

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 10
and finer pitch capability. They have become the standard for AI, HPC and other
advanced semiconductor applications. Cantilever probe cards use angled probes
extending from the PCB and are typically used in analog, driver IC and selected memory
applications where pin-count and pitch requirements are less demanding. The latest
generation of AI accelerators increasingly relies on MEMS (Micro-Electro-Mechanical

Systems) based vertical probe cards, which support ultra-fine pitches, higher pin counts
and improved probing precision. Technoprobe currently leads this segment with pitch
capability reaching approximately 40 micrometres.
For leading-edge AI applications, probe cards must increasingly support:
1. Higher current-carrying capability (CCC).
2. Stable contact resistance.
3. Lower parasitic inductance for signal integrity.
4. Greater durability under varying thermal conditions.

For CHPT, with their MEMS probe capabilities stemming from their smartphone
application processor probe card supply, they are in a good position to capture the
growing demand for MEMS probe cards as AI/HPC are shifting to MEMS probes. We also
expect probe card content per device to continue rising as AI accelerators grow in die
size, pin count, power consumption and packaging complexity. This should drive secular
growth for both probe card suppliers and upstream component providers, making chip

probing one of the most attractive areas within the semiconductor testing value chain.

Sockets
Semiconductor test sockets are electro-mechanical interfaces that are used post chip
probing and chip packaging process to test the functionality of the chips. Sockets are

placed on to load boards on top of testers during the final test/system level test
processes. Handlers place the chips onto the sockets, and pins inside the sockets enable
the testers to read the signals from the device under test (DUT, i.e. the packaged chips),
with tester signals fanning out  from beneath the PCB load board.
Figure 16: Socket illustration
Source: UBS

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 11
As AI accelerators become larger and more complex, socket requirements continue to
increase. High-end sockets typically comprise a socket housing, alignment plate,
integrated thermal-control components and contact elements that connect the DUT to
the tester. Spring probes (pogo pins) are the most common choice for HPC sockets, with
high durability, individual probe replacement flexibility, and consistent contact force. FT

and SLT sockets account for more than 70% of the total socket market, with burn-in
sockets representing the remainder.
Compared with the probe card market, the socket industry is more fragmented. Key
suppliers include:
 WinWay (6515.TW) : A major share gainer in AI/HPC sockets, with strong
exposure to Nvidia and AMD. The company specializes in high-performance
coaxial sockets and is developing its next-generation HyperSocket platform. It is
also expanding into probe cards through its partnership with Technoprobe.

 Leeno (058470.KQ) : Vertically integrated supplier focused on smartphone AP
and memory sockets.
 ISC (095340.KQ): Leading memory socket supplier with elastomer expertise.
 Smiths Interconnect (Acquired by Molex) : A key player in AI/HPC socket
industry, with its DaVinci coaxial sockets able to support high frequency
connectivity testing.
 Yamaichi (6941.T): Leading Japanese socket supplier with strong automotive
exposure.
 Yokowo (6800.T): Major provider of contact probes and socket pins.

 Cohu (COHU.O) : Broad test-solutions provider spanning ATE, handlers and
sockets, with greater auto/industrial/consumer  exposure.

AI accelerators are pushing socket requirements beyond 10,000 pins, making coaxial

sockets the industry standard for leading-edge AI/HPC devices. These sockets must
support transmission speeds of up to 224G while handling significantly higher thermal
design power (TDP) during testing.
Elastomer technology is increasingly being adopted alongside spring-probe (pogo-pin)
architectures to reduce chip damage and improve contact reliability. WinWay's next-
generation HyperSocket integrates elastomer materials between the DUT and spring

probes, improving probe lifetime, reducing cleaning requirements and enhancing
production efficiency. We believe HyperSocket could become an important
differentiator as next-generation AI accelerators demand higher pin counts, bandwidth
and thermal performance. We estimate HyperSocket could contribute approximately
5% of WinWay's revenue in 2027E while carrying a 20-30% ASP premium versus
conventional coaxial sockets.
The transition toward larger AI packages, higher pin counts and faster data-transfer

speeds should continue to increase socket content per device. We expect this trend to
support long-term growth in the socket market, with technology leaders such as
WinWay well positioned to gain share through next-generation solutions including

HyperSocket.

Load Board/Interface board
Load boards are specialised printed circuit boards (PCBs) that connect the tester to the
device under test (DUT) during final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). Acting as the
electrical interface between the packaged chip and automated test equipment (ATE),

load boards are responsible for power delivery and high-speed signal transmission
throughout the testing process. As AI and HPC devices become increasingly complex,
load board requirements continue to rise. Multilayer construction is crucial as modern
load boards often require more than 50 layers for smartphone application processors
and over 80 layers for leading-edge AI accelerators to support higher pin counts, dense
signal routing and greater power delivery demands. At the same time, impedance

control is an important factor as maintaining signal integrity is becoming increasingly
important as interconnect bandwidth rises and high-speed SerDes blocks become more
prevalent. These trends are driving greater technology content and value per load board.
We expect load board content to increase alongside AI chip complexity, larger package
sizes and higher interface speeds, benefiting suppliers with advanced multilayer PCB
capabilities such as CHPT.

Figure 22: CHPT's AP load board
Source: Company data (CHPT)
Figure 23: CHPT's AI load board
Source: Company data (CHPT)
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing
The industry's adoption of shift-left testing should drive a multi-year structural growth
opportunity for chip probing (CP) and its supply chain. As advanced packaging costs rise
and AI accelerators become increasingly complex, semiconductor manufacturers are
placing greater emphasis on identifying known-good dies (KGD) before packaging. This

### Page 13

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 13
underpins our above-consensus forecasts for Chunghwa Precision Test (CHPT), with
2027E/28E sales 7%/15% above consensus driven by structural AI/HPC probe card and
test consumables demand, capacity expansion, and increasing test interface content as
test insertions and test time increase. WinWay should also benefit through its growing
probe card business alongside partner Technoprobe, with probe card sales expected to

grow 94%/72% YoY in 2026E/27E following 161%/260% growth in 2024/25.
Figure 24: CP is set to grow substantially with  shift-left, each packaging step set to be MORE costly than the prior step

Source: UBS
The economics of advanced packaging reinforce this trend. Heterogeneous integration,
chiplet architectures and 2.5D/3D packaging significantly increase package value, while

defects identified late in the manufacturing process can lead to substantial scrap costs.
To mitigate this risk, foundries and OSATs such as TSMC and ASE employ distributed
testing flows incorporating KGD, known-good-stack (KGS) and assembly test screening,
to catch defects before the components enter a multi-week packaging cycle (>2 weeks
for CoW and 2 weeks for oS). The importance of these tests rises as AI clusters become
increasingly dependent on component reliability and low defective-parts-per-million

(DPPM) rates. Given the multiplicative yield degradation, a 300bps yield decline at just
one manufacturing stage (e.g. dropping from 99.5% to 96.5%) reduces overall
integrated four-stage yield from 98.0% to 95.0%; on a 500,000 unit ramp at US
$10,000 per unit, this 3% scrap delta translates into around US$150m in unrecoverable
losses given >6-month manufacturing lead times.
At the same time, rising chip power consumption is increasing the technical difficulty of

wafer-level testing. As chip thermal design power scales beyond 1,000W and power
density is expected to surge  another 8x by 2030, traditional wafer-level chip probe
(WLCP) encounters severe physical bottlenecks. Finer probe pitches and higher power
densities increase the risk of overheating, probe needle burnout and yield degradation,
creating demand for more advanced probing and thermal control solutions.

Hermes Testing (HTSI) is a key beneficiary of this trend. Leveraging its long-standing
relationship with TSMC and expertise in semiconductor test solutions, HTSI supplies
advanced thermal control modules used alongside leading-edge probers (made by
Tokyo Electron), particularly for AI applications. These thermal control modules are

### Page 14

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 14
required for high TDP semiconductor wafer probe in order to maintain the temperature
of the chuck to avoid needle burns or probe card damages. We estimate HTSI will ship
approximately 3,000 thermal-management modules in 2027E, up from 1,200 in 2026E,
supporting sales growth of 127%/152% in 2026E/27E. HTSI is also the partner for
prober installation, prober maintenance and services. In addition, HTSI provides high-

end chuck processing services, an area that should benefit from increasing package sizes
and higher wafer warpage associated with advanced packaging.
Beyond thermal control modules, HTSI also supplies consumables and customised
solutions for wafer-level testing, including probe card cleaning sheets and laser-based
probe head cleaning systems. As probing intensity increases under the shift-left testing
model, demand for cleaning and maintenance solutions should rise alongside probe

card utilisation. Currently, cleaning sheets is around 5% of sales,  but we believe it could
gain traction in 2027/28E and beyond. We see additional upside from future modular
laser cleaning systems that could be integrated directly with probers, further expanding
HTSI's content opportunity within advanced wafer-level testing.
Rapid capacity expansions accelerating growth
Industry test interface suppliers are expanding capacity aggressively to meet rising AI

and HPC demand. As advanced semiconductors require higher-performance probe
cards, sockets and interface boards, capacity additions are becoming increasingly
important to support growth across both wafer-level and package-level testing.
We believe CHPT and WinWay are well positioned to gain share within the high-end AI/
HPC test interface market. Taiwanese suppliers benefit from their proximity to leading
fabless customers and the growing preference for localised supply chains, particularly

for advanced testing applications where responsiveness and engineering support are
critical. Against a backdrop of tight industry supply, both companies have announced
significant capacity expansion plans over the next two to three years.
CHPT: capacity expansion supporting AI/HPC share gains
CHPT's existing capacity is effectively fully utilised and the company is pursuing both
near-term and long-term expansion. Near-term growth will come from leased facilities,

with one production expansion scheduled for August 2026 and a second in March
2027, doubling and tripling year-end 2025 capacity, respectively. Longer term, CHPT's
new Fab 3 facility in Pingzhen will significantly expand in-house PCB manufacturing and
wet-processing capacity, with mass production expected around Q428, expected to
double existing capacity when fully ramped.
Combined with increasing exposure to higher-value AI/HPC products, including turnkey

CPU probe cards and TPU FT/SLT/burn-in boards, we expect CHPT to deliver revenue
growth of 49%/75%/43% in 2026E/27E/28E.
WinWay: scaling for the next phase of AI demand
WinWay's capacity is expected to increase from 3.5mn pins per month at year-end 2025
to 6mn currently, reaching 9mn by year-end 2026 and 14mn by year-end 2027 through
expansions in Renwu, Kaohsiung. By 2027, approximately 60% of pogo-pin
requirements should be internally sourced, strengthening both supply security and

margins.
We estimate these capacity additions will support revenue growth of 105%/73%/71%
in 2026E/27E/28E.

Advanced packaging growth remains the key demand driver
Capacity expansion by test interface vendors is ultimately supported by continued

investment throughout the advanced packaging ecosystem, from providers such as
TSMC, ASE, Intel, and Amkor. During the Q226 earnings calls, TSMC raised its recent
2026 capex from US$52-56bn to US$60-64bn and ASE raised its 2026 capex from US
$8.5bn to US$10.5bn, with both companies citing robust demand supporting the capex
raise. We believe capex for the next two years will be even higher than 2026, implying
continuous growth in future years. With advanced packaging technologies getting

significantly more complex and with larger form factor, illustrated by the upcoming
ramp of both TSMC's CoPoS (chip-on-panel-on-substrate) and Intel's EMIB-T
(embedded multi-die interconnect bridge) packaging technologies, test interface
solution providers will benefit with more value added consumables (see our CoPoS vs
EMIB-T deep dive).
We expect test interface content growth to outpace unit growth. For instance, test
socket's ASP directly correlates with its pin counts - with the addition of 20-30% more

pins inside the sockets, socket ASP can grow 20-30% or more as the socket might
require significant cooling capabilities as the TDP of the DUT rises with its larger form
factor. Unlike prior semiconductor cycles, much of the current demand is driven by rising
test interface content per device rather than unit growth alone.
